        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  /** init gtm after 3500 seconds - this could be adjusted */
  setTimeout(initGTM, 500);
});

document.addEventListener('scroll', initGTMOnEvent);
document.addEventListener('mousemove', initGTMOnEvent);
document.addEventListener('touchstart', initGTMOnEvent);

function initGTMOnEvent(event) {
  initGTM();
  event.currentTarget.removeEventListener(event.type, initGTMOnEvent); // remove the event listener that got triggered
  
}

function initGTM() {
  if (window.gtmDidInit) {
    return false;
  }
  window.gtmDidInit = true; // flag to ensure script does not get added to DOM more than once.
  const script = document.createElement('script');
  script.type = 'text/javascript';
  script.async = true;
  // ensure PageViews is always tracked (on script load)
  script.onload = () => {
    dataLayer.push({ event: 'gtm.js', 'gtm.start': new Date().getTime(), 'gtm.uniqueEventId': 0 });  
  };
  script.src = 'https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtm.js?id=GTM-N48RN3R';
  document.head.appendChild(script);
}

<script>
    function sendToNative(stateCode, countryCode) {
        if (document.cookie.indexOf("NativeApp") != -1) {
              window.messageToNative({"eventName": "Location", "parameters": { "stateCode": stateCode, "countryCode": countryCode }});
        }
    }

const products = document.querySelectorAll("[data-product-target]");
const productLabels = document.querySelectorAll("[data-product-label]");
var tickerBg = document.getElementById("ticker-bg");
var ticker = document.getElementById("ticker");

products.forEach((product) => {
  product.addEventListener("click", () => {
    const target = document.querySelector(product.dataset.productTarget);
    productLabels.forEach((productLabel) => {
      productLabel.classList.remove("active");
    });
    products.forEach((product) => {
      product.classList.remove("active");
    });
    product.classList.add("active");
    target.classList.add("active");

    if (sportsProduct.classList.contains("active")) {
      sportsProduct.children[0].src =
        "https://scmedia.itsfogo.com/$-$/e939501fc3de45e38e0a73f78f3d5354.svg";
    } else {
      sportsProduct.children[0].src =
        "https://scmedia.itsfogo.com/$-$/a62cdf5353ef417e856b5b91fc48b61e.svg";
    }

    if (casinoProduct.classList.contains("active")) {
      casinoProduct.children[0].src =
        "https://scmedia.itsfogo.com/$-$/53de5d0e1b804ca79769c4378374a69e.svg";
    } else {
      casinoProduct.children[0].src =
        "https://scmedia.itsfogo.com/$-$/76cc1c8076584fb29b6e95c5a4c6082c.svg";
    }

    if (pokerProduct.classList.contains("active")) {
      pokerProduct.children[0].src =
        "https://scmedia.itsfogo.com/$-$/4518244a4a8541a68118fe71b45bee7c.svg";
    } else {
      pokerProduct.children[0].src =

});

const sportsProduct = document.querySelector(
  "[data-product-target='#sports']"
);
const casinoProduct = document.querySelector(
  "[data-product-target='#casino']"
);
const pokerProduct = document.querySelector(
  "[data-product-target='#poker']"
);
const racingProduct = document.querySelector(
  "[data-product-target='#racing']"
);

var width = window.innerWidth;

const randomizeBg = (array) => {
  var bg = array[Math.floor(Math.random() * array.length)];
  document.querySelector(".lp-hero").style.backgroundImage = bg;
  if ((width < 1280) & sportsProduct.classList.contains("active")) {
    document.querySelector(".mobile-bg").src = SPORTS_BGS_MOBILE[0];
  }
  if ((width < 1280) & casinoProduct.classList.contains("active")) {
    document.querySelector(".mobile-bg").src = CASINO_BGS_MOBILE[0];
  }
  if ((width < 1280) & pokerProduct.classList.contains("active")) {
    document.querySelector(".mobile-bg").src = CASINO_BGS_MOBILE[0];
  }
  if ((width < 1280) & racingProduct.classList.contains("active")) {
    document.querySelector(".mobile-bg").src = RACING_BGS_MOBILE[0];
  }
};

document.onload = randomizeBg(SPORTS_BGS);

const sportsTicker = () => {
  ticker.src =
    "https://scmedia.itsfogo.com/$-$/61c509597a1a4997aae50f1835296fc6.svg";
};

const casinoTicker = () => {
  ticker.src =
    "https://scmedia.itsfogo.com/$-$/98dc050074554bcd88eecbc37b13baf3.svg";
};

const pokerTicker = () => {
  ticker.src =
    "https://scmedia.itsfogo.com/$-$/deb9fa4ff39f466dacf026eb488a29bf.svg";
};

sportsProduct.addEventListener("click", () => {
  randomizeBg(SPORTS_BGS);
  sportsTicker();
});
casinoProduct.addEventListener("click", () => {
  randomizeBg(CASINO_BGS);
  casinoTicker();
});
pokerProduct.addEventListener("click", () => {
  randomizeBg(CASINO_BGS);
  pokerTicker();
});
racingProduct.addEventListener("click", () => {
  randomizeBg(RACING_BGS);
  sportsTicker();
});

const tabContent = document.querySelector("#sports.states-list");
console.log(tabContent);
const boxShadowElement = document.querySelector(".box-shadow");

tabContent.addEventListener("scroll", () => {
  const scrolledToBottom =
    tabContent.scrollTop + tabContent.clientHeight ===
    tabContent.scrollHeight;
  if (scrolledToBottom) {
    boxShadowElement.style.display = "none";
  } else {
    boxShadowElement.style.display = "block";
  }
});
